DIVIDENDS                                                                        股息

a final dividend of HK$0.05 per share for the year ended 31 December 2023        截至二零二三年十二月三十一日止年度末期股息
to shareholders of record as of 2 May 2024. The proposal is further subject to   每股 0.05 港元予於二零二四年五月二日名列本公
the approval of the shareholders at the forthcoming annual general meeting.      司股東名冊之股東的提案。此提案取決於即將召
                                                                                 開的股東周年大會上股東之批准。

COMMUNITY RELATIONS AND SOCIAL                                                   社區關係及社會責任
RESPONSIBILITY
Social responsibility remains an integral part of our values as a Company. We    社會責任依舊是我們公司不可或缺的價值觀。我
work to make a positive impact on our employees, their families, our business    們致力為僱員及其家屬、業務夥伴、社區及環境
partners, local communities, and the environment and ensure compliance           帶來正面影響，及確保遵守法例和規管要求。
with legal and regulatory requirements.

Since 2015, we have been publishing an Environmental, Social, and                自二零一五年，我們開始刊發《環境、社會及管
Governance (“ESG”) Report to provide the public with an appreciation of how      治（「ESG」）報告》，讓公衆更清楚瞭解我們在可
we respond to different ESG issues, guided by our Sustainability Committee.      持續發展委員會的指導下，如何應對不同的 ESG
The Company’s 2023 ESG Report will be available on our website no later          議題。本公司二零二三年的 ESG 報告將會於二零
than 28 March 2024.                                                              二四年三月二十八日前在本公司網站刊載。


Part of our commitment includes actively promoting responsible drinking          我們部分的承諾包括積極在公司內部及向公眾推
within the organization and by the general public, protecting the environment,   廣暢飲有責、保護環境以及符合或超過政府所制
and meeting or exceeding environmental standards set by the government.          定的環境標準。


HUMAN RESOURCES                                                                  人力資源

responsibilities to shareholders, society, and the environment. As such, we      境的責任均扮演關鍵的角色。因此，我們繼續實
continue to implement programs to improve their well-being and capabilities.     行各發展項目，致力提升他們的福祉和能力。


We invested in in-house training programs and maximized the utilization of       我們透過投資在內部的培訓計劃，並更廣泛地使
publicly available webinars to further enhance and develop knowledge, skills,    用網上公衆研討會，進一步加強及發展僱員職能
and experiences needed to fulfill their duties. We also organized smaller-       所需的知識、技能和經驗。我們也舉辦了較小規
scale company programs and activities to foster camaraderie within the           模的公司項目及活動以締造友善工作間。
organization.

CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT 主席報告


HUMAN RESOURCES (Continued)                                                           人力資源（續）
We regularly review our policies to ensure appropriate and sufficient                 我們定期審查政策，以確保已採取合適和充分的
measures are in place to provide our employees with a safe, secure, and               措施，為僱員提供安全、安穩和健康的工作間，
healthy workplace conducive to their physical well-being and personal and             有利於他們的身體健康，以及個人和職業發展。
career development.

        previous years.                                                                         何調整。

        Deferred tax assets and liabilities arise from deductible and taxable                   遞延稅項資產及負債乃分別來自資產及負債
        temporary differences respectively, being the differences between the                   項目於財務報表內之賬面值及其稅基所產生

        Provisions are recognised when the Group has a legal or constructive                     撥備會在本公司或本集團須就已發生的事件
        obligation arising as a result of a past event, it is probable that an outflow           承擔法律或推定義務，因而預期會導致含有
        of economic benefits will be required to settle the obligation and a                     經濟效益的資源外流，在可以作出可靠的估
        reliable estimate can be made. Where the time value of money is material,                計時確認。如果貨幣時間值重大，則按稅前
        provisions are determined by discounting the expected future cash flows                  利率對預期未來現金流量的現值計列撥備，
        at a pre-tax rate that reflects current market assessment of the time value              該稅前利率反映當前市場對貨幣時間價值的
        of money and the risks specific to the liability.                                        評估以及該負債的特定風險。

        Where it is not probable that an outflow of economic benefits will be                    如果含有經濟效益的資源外流的可能性較
        required, or the amount cannot be estimated reliably, the obligation is                  低，或是無法對有關數額作出可靠的估計，
        disclosed as a contingent liability, unless the probability of outflow of                便會將該義務披露為或然負債，但資源外流
        economic benefits is remote. Possible obligations, whose existence                       的可能性極低則除外。如果本集團的義務須
        will only be confirmed by the occurrence or non-occurrence of one or                     視乎某宗或多宗未來事件是否發生才能確定
        more future events are also disclosed as contingent liabilities unless the               是否存在，亦會披露為或有負債，但資源外
        probability of outflow of economic benefits is remote.                                   流的可能性極低則除外。

        Where some or all of the expenditure required to settle a provision                      如果負債計提準備的部分或全部支出預計將
        is expected to be reimbursed by another party, a separate asset is                       由另一方償還，則針對幾乎確定的預期償還

        certain. The amount recognised for the reimbursement is limited to the                   於負債計提準備的帳面金額。
        carrying amount of the provision.

        dealings with the entity.

2       Key accounting judgements and estimates                                       2     主要會計判斷及估計
        Impairment of non-current assets                                                    非流動資產之減值
        The Group reviews the carrying amounts of non-current assets at each                本集團於各報告日期檢討非流動資產的賬面
        reporting date to determine whether there is objective evidence of                  值，以確定是否存在減值的客觀證據。當減
        impairment. When indicators of impairment is identified, management                 值指標確定後，管理層確定各自資產或賺取
        determine the recoverable amounts of respective assets or cash-                     現金單位的可收回金額。可收回金額為公允
        generating units. The recoverable amount is the greater of its fair value           價值減銷售成本與使用價值兩者的較高者。
        less costs of disposal and value in use. In determining the fair value less         於釐定公允價值減銷售成本時，管理層會參
        costs of disposal, management refer to the observable market prices                 考相類資產的可觀察市場價格。於釐定使用
        for similar assets. In determining the value in use, expected cash flows            價值時，由使用資產或賺取現金單位產生的
        generated by the assets or cash-generating units are discounted to                  預期現金流需貼現至現值。過程中涉及對收
        their present value, which requires significant judgement relating to the           入預測、未來經營成本估計及貼現率的重大
        revenue forecasts, estimations of future operating costs and discount               判斷。本集團運用所有可用資料以確定可收
        rate. The Group uses all readily available information in determining an            回金額的合理概算。當可收回金額低於賬面
        amount that is a reasonable approximation of the recoverable amount.                值時，減值虧損於損益確認。然而，在現金
        When the recoverable amount is lower than the carrying amount, an                   流量估計中所採用假設的任何變動將增加或
        impairment loss is recognised in profit or loss. However, any change in             減少減值撥備虧損，並影響本集團的財務狀
        the assumptions adopted in the cash flow forecasts would increase or                況。非流動資產的性質和賬面值詳情分別於
        decrease the provision for impairment loss and affect the Group’s financial         附註 11 及 12 中披露。
        position. Details of the nature and carrying amounts of non-current assets
        are disclosed in notes 11 and 12.

                                        active markets for identical assets or                            市場之未經調整報價）計量之公允值
                                        liabilities at the measurement date

                                        1, and not using significant unobservable                         據）且並未使用重大不可觀察輸入數

24      Financial risk management and fair values                                       24    金融風險管理及公允價值
        Exposure to credit, liquidity, interest rate and currency risks arises in the         本集團在一般業務過程中存在信貸、流動資
        normal course of the Group’s business. The Group’s exposure to these                  金、利率及外匯風險。本集團面對該等風險

        Group to manage these risks are described below.                                      理政策及慣列，載述如下。

(a)     Credit risk                                                                     (a)   信貸風險
        Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will default on its                信貸風險指交易對手違約其合約責任導致本
        contractual obligations resulting in a financial loss to the Group. The               集團財務虧損的風險。本集團的信貸風險主
        Group’s credit risk is primarily attributable to trade receivables. The               要來自應收貿易賬項。本集團因現金及等同
        Group’s exposure to credit risk arising from cash and cash equivalents                現金項目而產生的信貸風險有限，因為交易
        is limited because the counterparties are banks for which the Group                   對手為本集團認為信貸風險較低的銀行。
        considers to represent low credit risk.

        The Group does not provide any guarantees which would expose the                      本集團並無因作出其他擔保而面對信貸風險。
        Group to credit risk.

        Trade receivables                                                                     應收貿易賬項
        The Group’s exposure to credit risk is influenced mainly by the individual            本集團的信貸風險主要受每名客戶的個別特
        characteristics of each customer rather than the industry or country in               徵影響，多於受客戶的經營所在地或行業的
        which the customers operate and therefore significant concentrations                  影響。因此，當本集團認為個別客戶有重大
        of credit risk primarily arise when the Group has significant exposure to             風險時，將會令信貸風險更為集中。於報告
        individual customers. At the end of the reporting period, 12% (2022: 29%)             期末，應收貿易及其他賬項總額的 12%（二
        and 34% (2022: 62%) of the total trade and other receivables was due from             零二二年：29%）及 34%（二零二二年：62%）
        the Group’s largest customer and the five largest customers respectively.             分別來自本集團最大客戶及五大客戶。

        The Group measures loss allowances for trade receivables at an amount                 本集團以終身預期信貸虧損的金額計量應收
        equal to lifetime ECLs, taken into account historical data, current                   貿易賬項的虧損撥備，並考慮到過往數據、
        conditions and the Group’s view of economic conditions over the                       現況及本集團對於在應收賬項預計年期內之
        expected lives of the receivables. Further quantitative disclosure in respect         經濟狀況的看法。本集團的應收貿易賬項產
